      "solution": "**Identify.** For a sample of perfect gas when its pressure is changed isothermally from $p_{i}$ to $p_{f}$, the entropy change is given by\n\n**Concept.** Apply the Chemistry General definition/stoichiometry from NCERT; the claim that holds is **$\\Delta S = nRT \\ln \\left\\lvert \\, \\frac{(p)}{p_{i}} \\right.$**.\n\n**Work.** Link the stem to the verified wording “$\\Delta S = nRT \\ln \\left\\lvert \\, \\frac{(p)}{p_{i}} \\right.$”, then reject each distractor that violates that fact or the matching formula.\n\n**Option scan.**\n✗ (1) $\\Delta S = nR \\ln \\left\\lvert \\, \\left( \\frac{p_{f}}{p_{i}} \\right) \\right.$\n✗ (2) $\\Delta S = nR \\ln \\mid (p)$\n✓ (3) $\\Delta S = nRT \\ln \\left\\lvert \\, \\frac{(p)}{p_{i}} \\right.$\n✗ (4) $\\Delta S = RT \\ln \\mid \\quad \\left(\\frac{p_{f}}{p_{i}}\\right)$\n\n**Hence proved.** The verified option wording is **(3) $\\Delta S = nRT \\ln \\left\\lvert \\, \\frac{(p)}{p_{i}} \\right.$**.\n\n**Eliminate.**\n✗ (1) $\\Delta S = nR \\ln \\left\\lvert \\, \\left( \\frac{p_{f}}{p_{i}} \\right) \\right.$ — Conflicts with “$\\Delta S = nRT \\ln \\left\\lvert \\, \\frac{(p)}{p_{i}} \\right.$”.\n✗ (2) $\\Delta S = nR \\ln \\mid (p)$ — Conflicts with “$\\Delta S = nRT \\ln \\left\\lvert \\, \\frac{(p)}{p_{i}} \\right.$”.\n✗ (4) $\\Delta S = RT \\ln \\mid \\quad \\left(\\frac{p_{f}}{p_{i}}\\right)$ — Conflicts with “$\\Delta S = nRT \\ln \\left\\lvert \\, \\frac{(p)}{p_{i}} \\right.$”.",
